## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单：westd-22162 cuda:0/1/2/3，不得用其他服务器或 GPU
- v6_random 实验用 v6 代码（含 per_problem_ii 输出），**不是** v5 代码——v5 random 结果已有但只有 segment-level
- 服务器代码已全局切 bf16（commit 20182ec），不要回退 fp16——fp16 在 Layer 27 产生 NaN 导致 n_paired 降至 ~20/50
- submit_training_job 的 command 必须自带 `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`，不能假设 .venv 存在
- Qwen 实验 QUEUED 但 GPU 被 v6_random 占着（cuda:0/1/3），**必须等 v6_random 完成才能跑 Qwen**——不要强行抢占
- 截稿 **EMNLP 2026 ARR 2026-05-25**，距今 11 天

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：**westd-22162**，4× RTX 6000 Pro 96G
- 工作目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/pacs`
- Python：`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base 环境）
- 环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- 模型路径：DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B（默认 HF cache）；Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 在 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct/`
- 数据/代码：`/root/autodl-tmp/pacs/`
- 活跃进程（reconcile 确认）：
  - v6_random_s015: PID 72073, cuda:0
  - v6_random_s020: PID 71488, cuda:1
  - v6_random_s025: PID 73459, cuda:3
- 活跃 cron：3 个（分别监控上述 3 个 RUNNING 实验）
- cuda:2 空闲（v6_random_s030 之前 failed，未重跑）
- 活跃 Worker：`worker_code_p0-per-problem-ii-fix_20260514T032447`（running，可能僵尸——已运行超 24h）；`worker_writing_paper-methodology_20260514T052759`（running，论文方法论写作）

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**：v6 importance 4 组全部 success + per-problem bootstrap CI 全部 p=0.0000 → D017 确认 importance gradient 12-75×。v6 random 3 组正在跑（s015/s020/s025），用于 problem-level permutation test 与 v6 importance 对照。

**Registry 摘要**：
- RUNNING: v6_random_s015 (cuda:0), v6_random_s020 (cuda:1), v6_random_s025 (cuda:3)
- QUEUED: qwen_importance_s015/s020/s025/s030（等 GPU 释放）
- v6_random_s030 failed（被误杀），cuda:2 空闲——可以重跑
- DONE(success): v6_importance 4 组, v5_random 4 组, mvp_v4_legacy 2 组, mvp_v4_random_r2
- 总计 33 实验：11 success / 7 negative / 12 failed / 3 killed

**等待中**：
- 3 个 v6_random cron 回调（完成后触发 on-experiment-done）
- 2 个 Worker 仍标记 running（可能需要检查是否僵尸）
- Qwen 4 组实验等 GPU 释放后提交

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- v6_random_s015/s020/s025 任一完成 → on-experiment-done 分析 per_problem_ii → 与对应 v6_importance 做 problem-level permutation test
- v6_random 3 组全完成 → 释放 cuda:0/1/3 → 立即提交 qwen_importance_s015/s020/s025（3 GPU 并行）；cuda:2 跑 v6_random_s030 重跑或 qwen_importance_s030
- **v6_random_s030 需要重跑**（cuda:2 现在空闲，应尽快安排）——这是 permutation test 的 s=0.30 对照组
- Qwen 4 组全完成 → 分析 cross-model replication 结果 → 若 Qwen 也观察到 phase-modulated severity → MVP Pass 标准 #2 达成 → 全力论文写作
- Qwen gradient < importance DeepSeek 但仍 >1× → 仍可写 "model-dependent magnitude but consistent direction"
- Qwen 无 phase-modulated pattern → negative，走 red-section framing 调整
- 论文写作 Worker 已在跑 methodology section——回调后审查质量

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心发现**：Importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 急剧放大（median gradient 12-75×），而 random 保持平坦（~1×）。这是 "emergent phase-strategy interaction"（Director framing 指令），**不是** "importance metric limitation"。

**决策链**：D010 MVP FAIL on reversal → D014 pivot to phase-modulated severity → D015 GO → D016/D017 bootstrap 确认 → 现在做 cross-model replication + 论文。

**易错点**：
- **不要把 v5 random（segment-level）和 v6 importance（per-problem-level）直接做 permutation test**——必须用 v6 random 的 per_problem_ii 才能做 problem-level 对比
- v6_random_s030 failed 是因为 bf16-nan-fix Worker 误杀 tmux session，不是代码问题——直接重跑即可
- Qwen 之前被 kill 是 GPU 抢占（SIGKILL），不是 OOM 也不是速度问题（~50s/problem，与 DeepSeek 相当）——D018 已纠正
- Crossover 现象（s=0.25 时 importance 在 high phase 优于 random 但在 low phase 劣于 random）是核心新颖发现，论文 contribution 之一
- 文献空白已由 3 个独立 Worker 确认：无先前工作报告 phase-dependent strategy crossover in compound sparsity
- cuda:2 空闲是失职状态——应立即安排 v6_random_s030 重跑

{
  "parentUuid": "f3cf9042-f6e4-4c0d-9d3d-43ef4c69d3b9",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Project Memory — phase-gated-compound-sparse\\n\\n## 关键决策与经验\\n\\n### D015 GO 判定依据 (2026-05-14)\\n- **Median 为主指标**：mean/median ratio 高达 43:1 (s=0.30 low phase)，极端重尾分布使 mean 不可靠\\n- **GO 标准**：importance median gradient >> random median gradient\\n- **实际数据**：importance 12.8×→28.7×→52.9×→74.9× vs random ~1× → 差异数量级级别\\n- **Sub-exponential 增长**：gradient 增长率递减 (2.24x→1.84x→1.42x)，描述为\\\"单调递增但亚指数\\\"\\n- **Crossover 现象**：s=0.25 时 importance 在 high phase 优于 random (0.584 vs 3.002)，但在 low phase 劣于 random (30.903 vs 2.915) — 这是新颖发现\\n\\n### Entropy-Discrimination Collapse 机制 (2026-05-14)\\n- Head pruning → surviving heads 注意力更均匀 → importance score 区分度崩溃\\n- Importance eviction 在 distributed-attention phases 退化为有偏伪随机\\n- 这解释了 crossover: high-sparsity phase 注意力集中→importance 有效; low-sparsity phase 注意力分散→importance 失效\\n\\n### Importance 指标三大修复 (2026-05-14, commit 20182ec)\\n1. `torch.where(mask.bool(), reshaped, torch.zeros_like(reshaped))` — 修复 inf×0=NaN\\n2. `importance /= torch.arange(seq_len, 0, -1, ...)` — 修复 causal attention 位置偏差\\n3. NaN layer skip — 修复 Layer 27 NaN 中毒\\n\\n### fp16 NaN 根因 (2026-05-14, n-paired 诊断)\\n- **根因**: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 在 fp16 + eager attention 下，Layer 27 约 8% logit 位置产生 NaN\\n- **影响**: 23/50 problems 始终丢失 low_sparsity 数据，n_paired 仅 16-22/50\\n- **验证**: bf16 下 0 NaN，确认是 fp16 数值不稳定\\n- **修复方案 A+B**: (1) torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16 根治; (2) PPL 计算中 NaN 过滤兜底\\n- **代码位置**: run_profiling.py L69 dtype, metrics.py L56 ppl_from_cached_losses, interaction_index.py L293 .float()\\n- **预期效果**: n_paired 从 ~20 提升到 ~45-50/50\\n- **注意**: 此问题同时影响 importance 和 random 实验，两者公平比较不受影响，但样本量减少\\n- **bf16 fix 已 apply 并验证通过** (2026-05-14)\\n\\n### NaN Overlap 诊断结果 (2026-05-14)\\n- **方法**: 对比 v6 importance 实验中 s015 vs s030 的 per_problem_ii null 模式，150 pairs (50 problems × 3 phases)\\n- **结论: OVERLAP_IMPERFECT** — 23/150 (15.3%) 不一致\\n  - ii_high: 3/50 不一致 (6.0%)\\n  - ii_mid: 12/50 不一致 (24.0%)\\n  - ii_low: 8/50 不一致 (16.0%)\\n- **含义**: Dense perplexity 在所有 level 间共享同一次 forward pass，不一致必然来自 sparse 条件在某些 level 下产生额外 NaN\\n- **存在非单调行为**: P0, P47 等中间 level null 但两端有值，排除\\\"sparsity 越高越容易 NaN\\\"的简单解释\\n- **选择偏差风险**: null-filter 后做条件间比较时，被过滤掉的 segment 可能正是 sparse 条件表现最差的位置\\n- **影响评估**: 主效应(gradient 12-75× vs ~1×)数量级差异大，selection bias unlikely to overturn，但论文应声明此 limitation\\n- **v6 importance 实验可能仍在 fp16 下运行**（bf16 fix apply 时间与 v6 启动时间不确定）——若需要最干净结论，建议 bf16 重跑\\n\\n### Qwen 速度纠正 (2026-05-14, Reviewer 核实)\\n- ~~之前诊断 37-75min/problem 是错误的~~\\n- **实际速度 ~50s/problem，与 DeepSeek 相当**\\n- Qwen kill 真实原因：被 v6_random 实验启动时挤占 GPU（SIGKILL），非 OOM 非速度问题\\n- Qwen 模型完整可用，4 GPU 并行 ~1h 可完成 4 组实验\\n\\n### v6_random_s030 误杀 (2026-05-14)\\n- bf16-nan-fix Worker 在操作 tmux 时误杀了 v6_random_s030 的 session\\n- 已创建 v6_random_s030_r2 重试 (cuda:2)\\n\\n### Framing 指导 (Director, 2026-05-14)\\n- 描述为 \\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\"，不是 \\\"importance metric limitation\\\"\\n- 不要 fallback 思维，不建议投次级会议\\n- 3 个条件：crossover 稳定、文献空白、机制解释\\n\\n### 服务器环境\\n- westd-22162: 4× RTX 6000 Pro 96G, conda base 环境\\n- 环境激活: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\\n- 数据/模型在 `/root/autodl-tmp/pacs/`\\n- Qwen2.5-7B 已下载: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct/`\\n- 服务器代码已切换到 bf16（2026-05-14 bf16-fix worker apply）\\n\\n### 文献空白确认 (2026-05-14)\\n- 3 个独立 Worker 确认无先前工作报告 phase-dependent strategy crossover in compound sparsity\\n- Ada-KV, NACL, DefensiveKV 等均未涉及此交叉现象\\n- 已入 literature_map.yaml (~15 条新条目)\\n\", \"total_chars\": 3301, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

{
  "task_type": "writing:paper-background",
  "project": "phase-gated-compound-sparse",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：撰写论文 Section 3 \"Background and Problem Setup\"\n\n你需要为一篇 EMNLP 2026 long paper 撰写 Section 3，约 1 页。\n\n### 论文核心发现\nCompound sparsity（attention head pruning + KV cache eviction）的 severity 不是均匀的——它是 strategy-dependent 且 phase-modulated 的。Importance-based eviction 在 low-sparsity reasoning phase 产生 12-75× severity amplification，而 random eviction 保持平坦（~1×）。\n\n### Section 3 结构（writing_plan.yaml）\n\n**3.1 Compound Sparsity Formulation**\n- 定义 compound sparsity = attention head pruning ⊗ KV cache eviction\n- Head pruning: 移除 fraction p 的 attention heads（基于 Frobenius norm importance）\n- KV cache eviction: 移除 fraction q 的 KV cache entries（基于 cumulative attention score 或 random）\n- Compound sparsity level s = effective sparsity when both applied simultaneously\n\n**3.2 Interaction Index (II)**\n- 定义 II 衡量 super/sub-additivity:\n  - PPL_d = dense (no sparsity) perplexity\n  - PPL_a = attention head pruning only perplexity\n  - PPL_k = KV cache eviction only perplexity\n  - PPL_b = both applied perplexity\n  - II = (PPL_b / PPL_d) - (PPL_a / PPL_d) - (PPL_k / PPL_d) + 1\n  - 即 II = r_both - r_attn - r_kv + 1 (其中 r_x = PPL_x / PPL_d)\n  - II > 0 → super-additive (compound 比 sum-of-parts 更糟)\n  - II < 0 → sub-additive\n  - II ≈ 0 → approximately additive\n- 这个归一化形式使得 II 在不同 problem/context 之间可比较\n\n**3.3 Phase Segmentation**\n- 基于 ThinKV 的 token-ratio 方法对推理 trace 进行 phase 分割\n- 定义 3 个 phase:\n  - high_sparsity: 推理初期，token 最少\n  - mid_sparsity: 中间阶段\n  - low_sparsity: 推理后期，token 最多（这是 compound interaction 最严重的阶段）\n- 使用 quantile-based boundaries（基于 generated token 的 cumulative ratio）\n- min_phase_length = 10 tokens 确保每个 phase 有足够统计意义\n- Research question: Is II constant across phases and strategies, or does it exhibit phase × strategy interaction?\n\n### 写作规范\n- 250 words per subsection, ~750 words total\n- 使用 LaTeX 格式（.tex 文件）\n- 数学公式用 \\equation 或 \\align 环境\n- 引用用 \\cite{key} placeholder\n- 不要 cherry-pick 或 oversell\n- 每个定量 claim 必须可追溯到实验\n- 文件写到本地路径: /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/artifacts/_project/paper/background.tex\n\n### 参考\n- SparK (spark-aaai2026): 首篇报告 compound sparsity 总是 super-additive\n- ThinKV (thinkv-iclr2026): phase 划分思想来源\n- Phase Transitions in LLM Compression (phase-transition-compression-2026): 不同压缩维度有独立 phase transition point\n\n先 read_project_file(file=\"writing_plan.yaml\", max_chars=0) 获取完整写作计划。\n然后用 Read 读已完成的 methodology.tex (/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/artifacts/_project/paper/methodology.tex) 确保术语和符号一致。\n写完后用 register_artifact 注册产出。",

eviction）的 severity 不是均匀的——它是 strategy-dependent 且 phase-modulated 的。Importance-based eviction 在 low-sparsity reasoning phase 产生 12-75× severity amplification，而 random eviction 保持平坦（~1×）。核心新颖性：phase-dependent strategy crossover in compound sparsity（无先前工作报告此现象）。\n\n### Section 2 结构（writing_plan.yaml）\n\n三条线索（每条约 1 段）：\n\n**(1) Compound/Joint Compression**\n- SparK (spark-aaai2026, AAAI 2026): 首篇报告 compound sparsity 总是 super-additive（ThinK+SnapKV 退化 65%）\n- SLiM (slim-icml2025, ICML 2025): 用 low-rank adapter 补偿 Q+S 聚合误差，证明误差非加性\n- UniComp (unicomp-2026): 40+ benchmarks 系统评估 compound compression\n- Compression Ordering (compression-ordering-2025): compound compression ordering 显著影响结果\n- Phase Transitions in LLM Compression (phase-transition-compression-2026): 不同压缩维度有独立 phase transition point\n- GRASPrune (graspune-acl2026, ACL 2026): 联合 pruning FFN+heads with learned gates\n- **Gap**: none analyzes phase × strategy interaction\n\n**(2) KV Cache Eviction**\n- Ada-KV, DefensiveKV (defensivekv-iclr2026, ICLR 2026), SnapKV, NACL\n- OBCache: OBD 理论推导 joint K-V saliency score\n- MUSTAFAR (mustafar-neurips2025): per-token magnitude pruning 70% sparsity 无损\n- RetentiveKV (retentivekv-2026): greedy bias in KV cache eviction\n- **Gap**: importance fragility noted but not phase-conditioned\n\n**(3) Phase-Aware Inference**\n- ThinKV (thinkv-iclr2026, ICLR 2026 Oral): phase 划分应用不同策略\n- PagedEviction (pagedeviction-eacl2026): structured block-wise KV cache pruning\n- Decision Representation Transitions (decision-repr-transition-2026): layer pruning 有 Silent/Decisive Phase 二分\n- Doing More With Less (doing-more-with-less-2026): unstructured pruning + TTS 可超越未剪枝\n- Sparse Frontier But wait — the figures need the v6 random data for the most up-to-date comparison. 

The v6 random experiments are still running, so I can't use that data yet. I'm weighing whether to spawn a figure-generation Worker now or wait, and I've already got 2 writing Workers running, so I need to be careful about over-spawning.

Looking at the writing plan, figures 1 and 2 can actually use the existing v5 data I have on hand, while tab2 needs the v6 bootstrap data. The constraint is that figure generation needs to run on the A6000 server using matplotlib, which affects how I schedule this work.

Since matplotlib doesn't require GPU resources, I can spawn a Worker for figure generation without interfering with the ongoing experiments. That would bring my Worker pool to 3 total. But before I do that, I should update report.md to document the current state and then wait for the v6_random experiment to finish and trigger the cron callbacks.
